# Indo-Aryan SIL surveys: joint etymology checkpoint

**Status: incomplete.** This is a saved, validated research checkpoint, not completion of the request to etymologise all surveys.

- Scope: 86,332 Indo-Aryan records in 37 SIL survey sources.
- Already linked at the start: 25,155.
- Newly saved: **28,944 rank-1 assignments on 28,425 records**, to 741 existing parents.
- Examined and held: **3,227 records**.
- Still unresearched: **29,525 records**. These are not being reported as tough or unclear; they remain unfinished work.

[Difficult-case audit](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/AUDIT.md) · [Exact held records](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/audit-records.csv) · [Unresearched inventory](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/unresearched-records.csv) · [Continuation state](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/STATE.md)

## Scope and method

The source inventory includes SIL survey citations and retains only Indo-Aryan language records, including Bote despite its current “Other” metadata clade. Retained Indo-Aryan comparison lists from Garo and Tripura surveys are included. The Halbi Webonary dictionary (`woods2019halbi`) was mistakenly included initially: its 7,773 dictionary-only records are now excluded, while its 41 saved links are retained as additional work (see scope-correction.json). Ten curated regional-family heads carrying survey citations are also excluded as non-attestations (see pass204-scope-audit.json); their actual survey members remain included. The Kalasha dictionary (`trail-cooper1999`), Gawri sounds/tones monograph (`gawri`), Kundal Shahi first-look working paper (`kund`) and non-Indo-Aryan Pat-Kolami records were excluded from this survey scope. See the [source inventory](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/source-inventory.json).

Candidate discovery invoked the current homepage `fetchLemmaList` implementation against the existing local database, followed by full CDIAL article and addendum inspection. The save shortlists use explicit form/sense sets; typographic normalization is only a discovery aid and does not change source records. Parent subsections, sound correspondences, known loans and ambiguous responses were checked before the accepted subsets were saved. This does not certify every pre-existing etymology in the survey corpus.

## Validation

- All 285 save batches passed `validate_assignments`, persistent-ID and exact target-content checks.
- Applying the newly saved rows across 285 temporary-graph checks changed 57,451 items (edges plus statuses); repeating every application changed zero.
- Intended rank-1 edges matched exactly. All unrelated overlay rows and graph edges were preserved.
- Shared compiled forms, edges and the identity registry remained byte-identical to their pre-save versions.
- The earlier focused assignment test run reported 16 passed, 1 pre-existing count assertion failed (2,603 versus 2,604). No claim of a clean full suite.
- No new lexical source or donor node was installed. Full ingestion/build/browser gates are inapplicable to this existing-node overlay edit; no compiled/browser database refresh or publication was requested or performed.

[Pass 1 verification](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/validation.json) · [Pass 2 verification](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/second-validation.json) · [Continuation verification](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/continuation-validation.json) · [Pronoun verification](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/fifth-validation.json) · [Latest joint verification](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/latest-joint-verification.json) · [Focused test evidence](/Users/aryamanarora/Documents/Code/jambu-all/data/curation/etymology-lab/indo-aryan-sil-20260914/test-results.json)
